  OBJECT-PROPERTIES
  {
    Date=27.07.17;
    Time=16:00:00;
    Version List=NAVW16.00,MYSW14.00;
  }
  PROPERTIES
  {
    OnInsert=BEGIN
               IF ("Cross-Reference Type No." <> '') AND
                  ("Cross-Reference Type" = "Cross-Reference Type"::" ")
               THEN
                 ERROR(Text000,FIELDCAPTION("Cross-Reference Type No."));

               Item.GET("Item No.");
               IF "Unit of Measure" = '' THEN
                 VALIDATE("Unit of Measure",Item."Base Unit of Measure");
               CreateItemVendor;

               // dynamic commerce ---
               WebshopManagement.SetWebshopItemModified("Item No.",FALSE,FALSE,FALSE);
               // dynamic commerce +++
             END;

    OnModify=BEGIN
               // dynamic commerce ---
               WebshopManagement.SetWebshopItemModified("Item No.",FALSE,FALSE,FALSE);
               // dynamic commerce +++
             END;

    OnDelete=BEGIN
               IF ("Cross-Reference Type" = "Cross-Reference Type"::Vendor) THEN
                 DeleteItemVendor(Rec);

               // dynamic commerce ---
               WebshopManagement.SetWebshopItemModified("Item No.",FALSE,FALSE,FALSE);
               // dynamic commerce +++
             END;

    OnRename=BEGIN
               IF ("Cross-Reference Type No." <> '') AND
                  ("Cross-Reference Type" = "Cross-Reference Type"::" ")
               THEN
                 ERROR(Text000,FIELDCAPTION("Cross-Reference Type No."));
               IF (xRec."Cross-Reference Type" = "Cross-Reference Type"::Vendor) THEN
                 DeleteItemVendor(xRec);
               IF ("Cross-Reference Type" = "Cross-Reference Type"::Vendor) THEN
                 CreateItemVendor;

               // dynamic commerce ---
               WebshopManagement.SetWebshopItemModified(xRec."Item No.",FALSE,FALSE,FALSE);
               WebshopManagement.SetWebshopItemModified("Item No.",FALSE,FALSE,FALSE);
               // dynamic commerce +++
             END;

  }
  CODE
  {
    VAR
      Text000@1000 : TextConst 'DEU=Sie k�nnen f�r eine leere Referenzart keine %1 eingeben.;ENU=You cannot enter a %1 for a blank Cross-Reference Type.';
      Text001@1001 : TextConst 'DEU=Diese %1 ist kein Barcode.;ENU=This %1 is not a bar code.';
      Item@1002 : Record 27;
      ItemVend@1003 : Record 99;
      "--- dynamic commerce ---"@1000000001 : Integer;
      WebshopManagement@1000000000 : Codeunit 5447300;

    PROCEDURE CreateItemVendor@1();
    BEGIN
      IF ("Cross-Reference Type" = "Cross-Reference Type"::Vendor) AND
         (ItemVend.WRITEPERMISSION)
      THEN BEGIN
        ItemVend.RESET;
        ItemVend.SETRANGE("Item No.","Item No.");
        ItemVend.SETRANGE("Vendor No.","Cross-Reference Type No.");
        ItemVend.SETRANGE("Variant Code","Variant Code");
        IF NOT ItemVend.FIND('-') THEN BEGIN
          ItemVend."Item No." := "Item No.";
          ItemVend."Vendor No." := "Cross-Reference Type No.";
          ItemVend."Variant Code" := "Variant Code";
          ItemVend."Vendor Item No." := "Cross-Reference No.";
          ItemVend.INSERT;
        END;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE DeleteItemVendor@2(ItemCrossReference@1000 : Record 5717);
    VAR
      ItemCrossReference2@1001 : Record 5717;
    BEGIN
      ItemCrossReference2.SETRANGE("Item No.",ItemCrossReference."Item No.");
      ItemCrossReference2.SETRANGE("Variant Code",ItemCrossReference."Variant Code");
      ItemCrossReference2.SETRANGE("Cross-Reference Type",ItemCrossReference."Cross-Reference Type");
      ItemCrossReference2.SETRANGE("Cross-Reference Type No.",ItemCrossReference."Cross-Reference Type No.");
      ItemCrossReference2.SETRANGE("Cross-Reference No.",ItemCrossReference."Cross-Reference No.");
      ItemCrossReference2.SETFILTER("Unit of Measure",'<>%1',ItemCrossReference."Unit of Measure");
      IF NOT ItemCrossReference2.FIND('-') THEN BEGIN
        ItemVend.RESET;
        ItemVend.SETRANGE("Item No.",ItemCrossReference."Item No.");
        ItemVend.SETRANGE("Vendor No.",ItemCrossReference."Cross-Reference Type No.");
        ItemVend.SETRANGE("Variant Code",ItemCrossReference."Variant Code");
        IF ItemVend.FIND('-') THEN
          REPEAT
            IF UPPERCASE(DELCHR(ItemVend."Vendor Item No.",'<',' ')) =  ItemCrossReference."Cross-Reference No." THEN
              ItemVend.DELETE;
          UNTIL ItemVend.NEXT = 0;
      END;
    END;

    BEGIN
    {
      --- dynamic commerce ---

      STANDARD-VERSION 2017

        FIELDS (0):
        TRIGGERS (4):

          OnInsert()
          OnModify()
          OnDelete()
          OnRename()

        KEYS (0):
        GLOBAL VARIABLES (1):

          NAME                          DATATYPE        SUBTYPE                       LENGTH

          WebshopManagement             Codeunit        "Webshop Management"


        TEXT CONSTANTS (0):
        FUNCTIONS (0):


      INDIVIDUELLE ANPASSUNGEN [Kundenname]

        [YYYY-MM-TT] - [Mitarbeiterk�rzel]:
        Anpassung, FIELDS (0):, TRIGGERS (0):, KEYS (0):, GLOBAL VARIABLES (0):, TEXT CONSTANTS (0):, FUNCTIONS (0)


      +++ dynamic commerce +++
    }
    END.
  }

    PROCEDURE PrintRecords@1(ShowRequestForm@1000 : Boolean);
    VAR
      ReportSelection@1001 : Record 77;
    BEGIN
      WITH ReturnShptHeader DO BEGIN
        COPY(Rec);
        ReportSelection.SETRANGE(Usage,ReportSelection.Usage::"P.Ret.Shpt.");
        ReportSelection.SETFILTER("Report ID",'<>0');
        ReportSelection.FIND('-');
        REPEAT
          REPORT.RUNMODAL(ReportSelection."Report ID",ShowRequestForm,FALSE,ReturnShptHeader);
        UNTIL ReportSelection.NEXT = 0;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE Navigate@2();
    VAR
      NavigateForm@1000 : Form 344;
    BEGIN
      NavigateForm.SetDoc("Posting Date","No.");
      NavigateForm.RUN;
    END;

    BEGIN
    {
      --- dynamic commerce ---

      STANDARD-VERSION 2017

        FIELDS (8):

          5447341      "Webshop Return Order Shop"         Code            10

        TRIGGERS (0):

          Webshop Order No.
          Webshop Return Order No.

        UNTIL ReportSelection.NEXT = 0;
      END;
    END;

OBJECT Table 7002 Sales Price

  OBJECT-PROPERTIES

    Date=27.07.17;
    Time=16:00:00;
    Version List=NAVW16.00,MYSW14.00;
  }
  PROPERTIES
  {
    OnInsert=BEGIN
               IF "Sales Type" = "Sales Type"::"All Customers" THEN
                 "Sales Code" := ''
               ELSE
                 TESTFIELD("Sales Code");
               TESTFIELD("Item No.");

               // dynamic commerce ---
               WebshopManagement.SetWebshopItemModified("Item No.",TRUE,TRUE,TRUE);
               // dynamic commerce +++
             END;

    OnModify=BEGIN
               // dynamic commerce ---
               WebshopManagement.SetWebshopItemModified("Item No.",TRUE,TRUE,TRUE);
               // dynamic commerce +++
             END;

               // dynamic commerce ---
               WebshopManagement.SetWebshopItemModified("Item No.",TRUE,TRUE,TRUE);
               // dynamic commerce +++
             END;

               IF "Sales Type" <> "Sales Type"::"All Customers" THEN
                 TESTFIELD("Sales Code");
               TESTFIELD("Item No.");

               // dynamic commerce ---
               WebshopManagement.SetWebshopItemModified("Item No.",TRUE,TRUE,TRUE);
               WebshopManagement.SetWebshopItemModified(xRec."Item No.",TRUE,TRUE,TRUE);
               // dynamic commerce +++
             END;

               ENU=Sales Price];

                                                                        Cust.GET("Sales Code");
                                                                        "Currency Code" := Cust."Currency Code";

                                                                        Campaign.GET("Sales Code");

                                                                        "Ending Date" := Campaign."Ending Date";

    OnInsert=BEGIN
               IF "Sales Type" = "Sales Type"::"All Customers" THEN
                 "Sales Code" := ''
               ELSE
                 TESTFIELD("Sales Code");
               TESTFIELD(Code);

               // dynamic commerce ---
               CASE Type OF
                 Type::Item: WebshopManagement.SetWebshopItemModified(Code,TRUE,TRUE,TRUE);
                 Type::"Item Disc. Group": BEGIN
                   "Last Date Modified" := CURRENTDATETIME;
                 END;
               END;
               // dynamic commerce +++
             END;

    OnModify=BEGIN
               // dynamic commerce ---
               CASE Type OF
                 Type::Item: BEGIN
                   WebshopManagement.SetWebshopItemModified(Code,TRUE,TRUE,TRUE);
                 END;
                 Type::"Item Disc. Group": BEGIN
                   "Last Date Modified" := CURRENTDATETIME;
                 END;
               END;
               // dynamic commerce +++
             END;

    OnDelete=BEGIN
               // dynamic commerce ---
               CASE Type OF
                 Type::Item: WebshopManagement.SetWebshopItemModified(Code,TRUE,TRUE,TRUE);
                 Type::"Item Disc. Group": BEGIN
                   WebshopUpdateManagement.DeleteSalesLineDiscount(Rec);
                 END;
               END;
               // dynamic commerce +++
             END;

    OnRename=BEGIN
               IF "Sales Type" <> "Sales Type"::"All Customers" THEN
                 TESTFIELD("Sales Code");
               TESTFIELD(Code);
             END;
